7～9 月期実質GDP は年率＋1.0％と 
プラス成長を予想（1 次QE）

○ 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比＋0.2％（年率＋1.0％）と、4～6月期（前期比＋
0.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半期連続でのプラス成長を予想 
○ 個人消費が伸び悩む一方、設備投資や民間在庫が押し上げる見込み。なお、公的在庫と輸入では
中東情勢を主因とする一時的な変動が生じ、成長率にプラス・マイナス両面で影響 
○ 年度後半の日本経済は、小幅なプラス成長を予想。インフレ再加速を受け消費抑制が続く一方、設
備投資が堅調に拡大。日本銀行は12月会合で政策金利を1.50％に引き上げる見込み

7～9月期の実質GDP（1次
速報値）はプラス成長 を
予想 
11月16日 （月） に、 内閣府から2026年7～9月期の 「四半期別GDP速 報（QE）」
（1次速報値）が公表される。現時点で入手可能な基礎統計の結果を踏まえ
ると、7～9月期の実質GDPは前期比＋0.2％（ 年 率＋1.0％） となる見込みだ。
4～6月期の前期比＋0.4％（年率＋1.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半
期連続でプラス成長になると予想する（図表）。 
7～9月期は、内需の柱である個人消費が伸び悩む一方、 設備投資の拡大や、
製造業を中心とする在庫積み増しが成長率を押し上げるとみられる。その他
では、前期の備蓄原油放出の反動が公的在庫投資のプラス要因として現れる
が、原油の代替調達進展を反映した輸入の増加がマイナス要因になるなど、 
図表 2026 年7～9 月期GDP（1 次速報）予測

前期比に対する寄与度（％Pt）

### Page 2

2

中東情勢による一時的な変動も引き続き成長率に影響する見込みだ。以下、
需要項目別に7～9月期の経済動向を詳しく確認しよう。 
個人消費は伸び悩み 。耐
久財の特需剥落や、 根強
い節約志向が影響 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％（4～6月期：同＋0.0％） と、2四半期連続で伸び
悩む結果になろう。消費の弱さの背景には、 複合的な要因があると考えられ
る。 
耐久財では、自動車とエアコンの「特需」剥落が押し下げ要因になろう。
自動車は、購入時にかかる自動車税の環境性能割が3月末に廃止されたこと
を受け、4月から自動車販売が過去平均に比べ上振れていたが、8月以降は再
び過去平均並みの水準に戻っている。エアコンは、2027年4月の省エネ基準
引き上げで販売価格が上昇するとの見方から駆け込み需要が生じていたが、
景気ウォッチャー調査のコメントなどをみると、8月に入った後は一服した
模様だ。9月は気温が低かったほか、長雨による客足の減少もエアコンなど
季節家電の売り上げに悪影響を及ぼしたとみられる。 
食品やエネルギー、 各種消耗品などの非耐久財では、物価高を背景とする
根強い節約志向により、 弱含みが続いているようだ。今回のインフレ局面が
本格化する前の2020年と比較すると、非耐久消費財の物価は足元までの約6
年半でおよそ1.3倍の水準に上昇した計算になる。こうした累積的な価格上
昇が、 消費者の体感物価の高まりを通じて節約志向を強め、 購入数量の抑制
につながっている可能性がある。 
サービス消費は、 イベント関連支出などで底堅さがみられる一方、日常的
な支出には物価高による節約志向が波及し、 サービスや価格帯、 顧客層によ
る二極化が進みつつあるとみられる。 また、8～9月には台風・長雨による旅
行・ 宿泊のキャンセルや外出控えの影響から、 レジャー関連を中心に消費が
伸び悩んだようだ。 
設備投 資は3四半期ぶり
に前期比プラス 
設備投資は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.9％） と、3四半期ぶりに前期
比プラスへ転じると予想する。 
直近の設備投資関連指標をみると、資本財の国内向け供給量を表す資本財
総供給指数（除く輸送機械）が7～8月に大きく上昇した。また第3次産業活
動指数では、ソフトウェア業の実質売上高が同じく7～8月に増加したことが
確認でき、どちらも7～9月期の設備投資が拡大したことを示唆している。 
加えて、前期の4～6月期に発生した一時的な押し下げ圧力（特許権の海外
譲渡による国内研究開発投資の減少） がなくなったこともプラス要因だ。た
だし、 これは裏を返せば、7～9月期のQEにおける設備投資の伸びが実態以上
に強い数字になるということでもあり、評価する際には留意が必要である。 
住宅投資は 前期比マイナ
スが継続 
住宅投資は前期比▲0.8％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.6％ ）と、2四半期連続で前期
比マイナスとなろう。中東情勢を背景とする資材不足は徐々に緩和している
とみられるが、7～8月の住宅着工件数が伸び悩んでおり、住宅価格や金利上

### Page 3

3

昇による需要低迷に加えて、 足元では地震や台風などによる着工 ・ 進捗の遅
れが発生している可能性がある。 
民間 在庫は 2四半期連続
で成長率にプラス寄与 
民間在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は、＋0.2％Pt（4～6月期：
＋0.3％Pt）とプラスが続く見込みだ。AI関連需要の強さなどを背景に、製
造業で在庫積み増しの動きが進んでいることが影響すると考えられる。な
お、4～6月期は石油化学製品などの供給不足を懸念した前倒し発注が在庫を
押し上げたとみられるが、7～9月期は原油の代替調達進展に伴って供給不足
が緩和したことで、そうした要因の影響は一巡した模様だ。 
公的需要 は備蓄原油放出
の反動で前期比プラスに 
公的需要は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.8％）と、2四半期ぶりの前期
比プラスを予想する。 
最大の要因は、公的在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度がプラスに
転じるとみられることだ。4～6月期は国家備蓄原油の放出により公的在庫変
動の寄与度が▲0.5％Ptと大幅マイナスになったが、7～9月期は国家原油備
蓄の減少が小幅なものにとどまり（6月末：105日分→9月末：102日分）、公
的在庫の減少ペースが緩和することで、成長率に対し＋0.3％Pt程度のプラ
ス寄与になると見込まれる。 
その他の公的需要項目では、政府消費を前期比＋0.2％（4～6月期：同＋
1.7％）と予想する。4～6月期は高校授業料・小学校給食費の無償化開始に
より高い伸びになったが、7～9月期はそうした特殊要因が剥落し、緩やかな
増加ペースに戻ると考えられる。公共投資は前期比▲0.5％（4～6月期：同
▲0.5％）と、土木関連工事の減少を受けて2四半期連続のマイナスを見込む。 
輸出が小幅に減少する 一
方、 輸入が増加し、外需は
4四半期ぶりのマイナス
寄与 
外需では、輸出が前期比▲0.3％（4～6月期：同＋0.4％）と小幅に減少す
る一方、輸 入が同＋1.3％（4～6月 期：同▲1.7％）と増加に転じる見込みだ。
実質GDP成長率に対する外需の寄与度は▲0.3％Ptと、4四半期ぶりのマイナ
スが予想される。 
輸入の増加は、原油の代替調達進展が主因だ。中東情勢の緊迫化により4
～6月期は一時的に原油輸入が急減したが、7～9月期は米国産原油やホルム
ズ海峡を通過しない中東産原油の調達が増え、9月を除いて前年並みの輸入
量が確保されている。 
輸入はGDPの控除項目であることから、7～9月期は輸入増加が成長率の下
押し要因になる。 ただし、代替調達の進展に伴って国家備蓄原油の放出が抑
制され、 先述したように7～9月期は公的在庫変動が成長率にプラス寄与する
とみられることから、輸入増による成長率押し下げ効果の一部は公的在庫変
動により相殺されるだろう。 
輸出では、前期の4～6月期に特許権の海外譲渡がサービス輸出を押し上げ
た （これは4～6月期の設備投資の押し下げ要因でもある） 反動が現れ、 一時
的に下振れするだろう。一方、旺盛なAI関連需要が支えになり、半導体・電

### Page 4

4

子部品や半導体製造装置などの財輸出は堅調に推移する見込みだ。インバウ
ンド需要 （非居住者家計の国内での直接購入） は、 日中対立の継続による中
国人訪日客の少なさに加え、 航空運賃上昇を受けた欧米訪日客の伸び悩みに
より、低調な結果になると見込まれる。 
2026 年度後半 の実質 GDP
は小幅プラス と予想 。個
人消費が伸び悩む一方、
設備投資が堅調拡大 
以上のとおり、7～9月期の日本経済は前期から減速するものの、4四半期
連続でプラス成長になると予想する。GDPの約半分を占める個人消費の伸び
悩みが足を引っ張っているが、設備投資や製造業を中心とする在庫積み増し
がけん引する格好だ。 
先行きについてみると、2026年度後半（2026年10～2027年3月）の 実質GDP
は、 均してみれば小幅なプラス成長になると予想する。個人消費が振るわな
い一方で、 設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が続くだろう。2026年度全体の
実質GDP成長率は、前年度末の堅調な伸びで今年度の平均水準が押し上げら
れることもあり（いわゆるプラスのゲタ）、前年比＋1％程度とまずまずの
成長率になる計算だ。 
年度後半の個人消費では、インフレ再加速による実質賃金の伸び悩みが消
費拡大の抑制要因になるだろう。 原油 ・ ナフサ価格高騰の影響が徐々に波及
し、 コア消費者物価指数 （生鮮食品を除く総合） は年度末にかけて前年比＋
3％程度まで上昇すると予想される（ただし、2026年末から政府の電気・ガ
ス料金支援策が実施されれば、0.4～0.5％Pt程度の押し下げ要因になる可能
性がある）。加えて、2027年4月の消費税減税開始前には、保存食品などを
中心とした買い控えの動きが生じるとみられ、 一時的に消費が落ち込む可能
性がある。 
他方で、年度後半の設備投資は堅調に拡大するだろう。日銀短観（9月調
査） では、2026年度の設備投資計画 （ソフトウェア・研究開発を含む設備投
資額 （除く土地投資額）） が前年比＋9.3％と高い伸びを維持した （6月 調 査：
同＋8.8％）。生産・営業用設備投資判断DIが2025年度後半以降に低下し、
設備の不足感が強まっていることをあわせて考えれば、 設備投資は今後拡大
傾向で推移していく見込みだ。 
また、政府の投資支援策である「大胆な投資促進税制」 （特定生産性向上
設備等投資促進税制）が2026年7月末から開始されたことも、今後の設備投
資にとってプラス要因になろう。同税制における減税規模の政府見込みを参
考に試算すると、2026～2028年度に設備投資の前年比変化率を約0.1％Ptず
つ押し上げる効果があると考えられる。 
ただし、 日銀が来年にかけて政策金利を一段と引き上げ、 企業の新規借入
金利がさらに上昇すると予想されること、 また、 投資財の価格が上昇傾向を
続けると考えられることを踏まえると、 中小企業を中心に設備投資を控える
企業が今後増加する可能性がある点には注意が必要だ。

### Page 5

5

日銀は10月会合で 政策金
利を据え置き 。次回利上
げは12月と予想 
10月29・30日に開催された金融政策決定会合において、 日本銀行は政策金
利を現行の1.25％で据え置いた。次の利上げ（1.25％→1.50％） は、次回12
月17・18日の会合と予想する。 前回利上げを行った9月会合に続いて 「3カ月
に1回」ペースでの利上げになり、 日銀の利上げペースが6月以前の 「半年強
に1回」から加速していることを印象付けるものになろう。 
企業の価格転嫁行動は一段と積極化しており、植田総裁が9月会合後の記
者会見で 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明したように、日本銀行は「物価引
き上げ」 から 「物価上振れ抑制」 へと金融政策運営の舵を切っている。12月
の追加利上げ後も、 金利上昇の影響を確認しながら、 日銀は最終的に2027年
半ばにかけて政策金利を2.0％まで引き上げていくだろう。 
なお、経済見通しの詳細については、みずほ総合研究所調査部「2026年・
2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ─ 転換点は近いのか、投資主導経済の行方
を探る ─」（10月2日）をご覧いただきたい。 
（https://www.mizuhobank.co.jp/corporate/mhri/research/report/pdf/outlook261002.pdf）

上週重要市場資訊
 隨著法國距離下屆總統大選僅剩六個月，極左翼與政府間的政治對立升溫，然而
法國政府急需推動預算案以控制財政赤字與龐大債務。在內政危機加劇與工會
預告 11月大罷工的背景下，市場對法國債務可持續性的擔憂升溫，將帶動法德
公債利差維持高檔，進而推升歐元區主權風險溢酬並拖累歐元表現。
 上週路透最新調查顯示，在 100 位受訪經濟學家中，高達 94%預期聯準會將於
10 月會議維持利率在 3.75%-4.00% 不變，惟全員一致預估 12 月將重啟升息。
儘管美國受 AI 投資熱潮帶動、經濟成長強勁，且美伊戰事推升油價突破百元
大關、加徵關稅使 PCE 通膨至少在 2028 年前難以回到 2% 目標，官員仍傾
向審慎行事。不過逾四成專家警告，聯準會恐落後通膨形勢，後續面臨至少再升
息兩次甚至擴大升息幅度的上行風險，預期美元大幅回檔的空間小，逢低承接買
盤依然強勁。
 路透最新調查顯示，受日圓疲軟與中東戰事推升能源及進口成本、加劇通膨壓力

影響，近七成受訪經濟學家預計日本央行將於 12月再度升息至 1.50%，延續 6
月與 9 月以來的較快緊縮步伐；同時，逾半數專家預估終端利率將上看 2.00%
或更高。不過，高達近四分之三受訪者警告，若高市早苗內閣提名的鴿派委員再
次投下反對票，恐壓抑日圓表現。在歐美央行年底前亦可能升息的背景下，日本
央行維持較快的緊縮節奏，有助於防止美日、歐日利差進一步擴大，避免日圓在
年底遭遇新一輪劇烈拋售，為日圓提供實質的下檔保護。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.360，低點在 32.704，以 32.508作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，台幣跳空開低，跌破今
年 7 月的低點 32.528；隨著台股表現不佳，外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣續創低位，
最低來到 32.704周內低點；而後台股低檔反彈，外資由匯出轉為雙向進出，加以出
口商積極進場拋售美金，台幣由貶轉升至 32.500下方；臨近周末，市場擔憂颱風來
襲周五可能休市，增添連續假期變數，外資再度大幅賣超台股並匯出，令台幣又跌
回 32.600上方，終場接在央行調節之下收在 32.508。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。近期全球科技股因 AI 類股估值偏高及融資風險疑慮
而持續修正，市場風險偏好降溫，股市面臨修正，外資連續賣超台股並匯出，台幣
走勢持續面臨壓力。另一方面，美伊戰事和談進展的消息反覆，令國際油價節節攀
升，突破每桶 110美元上方，引發通膨升溫的擔憂，支撐利率維持在高檔的預期，
美元指數也在 101.5 上方高位震盪，將不利台幣的表現。盤勢上來看，外資隨著股
市回檔修正表現偏向賣超台股並匯出，台幣偏弱，不過本周適逢月底，預計匯價逢
高仍持續有出口商拋售美金的需求，將限制台幣走貶的幅度。綜合以上，預估本週
台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣走升。周初中國公布9月工業生產物價指數 5.5%，表現優於預期的
5.4%(前值 5.2%)，且第三季 GDP表現 4.5%，也優於預期的 4.4%(前值 4.3%)，基本
面穩健支撐人民幣升破 6.6800 整數關口；不過周間美元指數持續於 101.5 上方高位
震盪，加以美伊戰事未見緩解，國際油價持續飆升，突破每桶 110 美元上方，人民
幣於 6.6800下方高檔震盪；臨近周末，人行中間價格連續走升，升破6.7200整數關
口，週五最高來到 6.7180，顯示中國人行允許人民幣溫和升值的走勢，終場境外人
民幣升破 6.6700整數關口 ， 收在6.6690， 週線升值160點 ， 境內人民幣則收在6.6704，
週線升值 166 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價持續偏升，將持續帶
動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現；就國際市場來看，最
新利率期貨顯示 ， 本周聯準會利率決議有9成的機率維持利率在 3.75%~4.00%不變 ，
不過年底前仍有一碼的升息空間，加以地緣政治風險未見緩解，能源價格高檔震盪
推升通膨與利率預期，美元指數維持在 101.5 上方高檔震盪，將限制人民幣升值的
速度跟幅度；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價
維持在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預
估本週人民幣偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6300-6.6900

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/27/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌。週初，USD/JPY 大致在 158 的整數關卡上下波動；
週間，AI相關概念股遭拋售，加之中東局勢緊張推升避險需求，10年期美債殖利率
跌破 5%，避險資金亦流入日圓，帶動 USD/JPY 下行最低來至 156.67；臨近週末，
市場避險需求持續加之日本 9 月通膨數據高於日本央行 2%目標，USD/JPY 一度下
跌至 156.06，後逢低買盤進場跌幅有所收斂，終場收在 156.86，週線下跌 113點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下： 一、美國10 月 S&P製造業 PMI
為 56.0高於 9 月的 55.9且為 53個月高點，服務業則為 57.9低於 9月的 58.8，綜合
PMI為 57.6亦低於 9 月的 58.4，顯示美國商業活動雖略有放緩但仍處於擴張，經濟
基本面依然穩健將持續支撐美元；此外，中東局勢惡化使能源供應風險上升，布蘭
特原油價格突破 116美元，為六個月以來高點，也將有利USD/JPY上行。二、日本
9 月全國整體消費者物價指數（CPI）年增 2.1%，且 9 月核心 CPI年增 2.1%，為九
個月以來首次超過日本央行 2%的目標，強化市場對日本央行短期升息的預期，預計
為日圓帶來支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY匯價處於各天期均線之
下且接連跌破 158 及 157 關卡，後在 156 整數關卡暫時獲得支撐，顯示逢低仍有買
盤進場，將限縮下行幅度。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較
高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.00-158.50

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
         Oct/27/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD震盪下跌。週初，EUR/USD在 1.1200整數關卡窄幅波動；週間
受到油價及美債殖利率回落，外加法國社會局勢略見緩解影響，帶動匯價反彈至
1.1260遇到賣壓後回落，後續大致在1.1190至 1.1250間波動；臨近週末，歐元區10
月份 PMI表現低迷，且市場對於法國政局和財政赤字擔憂持續，拖累 EUR/USD轉
若，匯價一度下行來至 1.1183，終場收在 1.1202，週線下跌 37點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏空，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元區10月 PMI
顯示德、法表現分化，法國製造業及服務業均表現疲弱低於50榮枯線，拖累歐元區
10 月綜合 PMI 由 9 月 53.1 下滑至 51.9，為四個月以來新低，經濟基本面承壓將不
利 EUR/USD上行；此外，法國社會及政經局勢不穩導致法債殖利率突破5%，市場
對其財政及政治危機擔憂持續，也將不利歐元表現。二、美國上週初請失業金人數
降至 19.4萬人，為三個月以來新低，顯示勞動力市場依然穩健，且10月 S&P製造
業 PMI為 56.0續創 53個月新高，服務業及綜合PMI維持穩健，顯示美國經濟仍具
韌性，預期將限縮 EUR/USD上行空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
各天期均線維持空頭排列，且匯價持續承壓於月線之下，顯示短線空方趨勢仍未改
變，在能有效突破上方月線前仍以震盪偏空看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週
呈現震盪偏空的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1120-1.1270

新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.19-115.10.22  
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.825%  
30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68%  
  公債－10年期- A15109 2.000%~2.002% 
      － 5 年期- A15108     1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，儘管市場預期強烈颱風「薔琵」來襲，週五金融市場可能
因颱風假而暫停交易，銀行間多預留帳上資金以應不時之需，但市況仍屬充
裕，即便有新增調度需求，也很容易滿足，令短率朝區間低位移動。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.68%，利率低位較上週下跌約 1 個基本點。

二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 週四於等殖系統成交在 2.002%，
5 年期指標券 A15108則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.88%。 
週二 10 年期公債標售得標利率 2.001%，落於市場預期區間下緣，並創 2008
年以來新高，顯示市場持續反映經濟成長與通膨預期。 
考量通膨前景及國際債市走勢仍具不確定性，市場觀望氣氛濃厚；惟第四季公
債供給相對有限，可望抑制殖利率大幅彈升。預期短線台債殖利率維持區間整
理，5 年券及 10 年券交易區間分別估於 1.82%~1.84%及 1.98%~2.01%。

### Page 1

Summary: Our first take on Unimicron’s 3Q26 results (released on 27 October) is that 
earnings were better than expected, driven by stronger-than-expected non-operating 
gains. We attribute the upside primarily to a smaller-than-feared valuation loss on UMC in 
3Q26. In addition, the company mentioned that other investments, including Unimicron 
shares held through investment vehicles, contributed c.TWD3bn of mark-to-market gains in 
3Q26. As for the core business, 3Q26 revenue reached TWD52.7bn (up 23% QoQ, up 
55% YoY), with gross margin expanding 4.0ppt QoQ to 28.8% and operating margin rising 
4.5ppt QoQ to 20.0%, supported by improving profitability in ABF substrates and HDI. We 
believe the profitability performance was broadly in line with the latest buy-side 
expectations. Looking ahead, while the company does not provide numerical guidance, 
revenue and gross margin should continue to improve in 4Q26. Given Unimicron’s leading 
position across most CSP AI ASIC projects, Nvidia GPUs, and EMIB-T, we believe the 
company should continue to benefit from a favourable pricing environment amid the 
current supply-demand dynamics and ongoing specification upgrades, including larger 
substrate body sizes and technology migration. We have a Buy (1) rating on Unimicron 
with a 12M TP of TWD1,500, based on a target PER of 35x applied to our 1-year-forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected demand.

Source: Company data, Daiwa forecasts, Bloomberg

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take on Unimicron’s 3Q26 results (released on 27 October). We will 
review our model in due course.

• 3Q26 results beat expectations. Unimicron’s 3Q26 earnings came in at TWD10.1bn 
(EPS: TWD6.15), beating our estimate of TWD4.2bn and Bloomberg consensus of 
TWD6.7bn by 140% and 49%, respectively, mainly due to higher-than-expected non-
operating gains. In our previous note, Unimicron recognised c.TWD8bn of mark-to-
market gains on UMC (2303 TT, TWD122.5, Neutral [3], covered by Rick Hsu) in 2Q26. 
We had originally expected Unimicron to recognise c.TWD5bn of valuation losses in 
3Q26, given UMC’s share-price performance in July. However, the actual valuation loss 
of c.TWD0.7bn was substantially better than feared. In addition, the company attributed 
part of the strength to c.TWD3bn of other mark-to-market gains, including gains related 
to Unimicron shares held through its investment vehicles. This is broadly consistent with 
the reported 3Q26 total non-operating contribution of c.TWD2.6bn after accounting for 
the UMC valuation loss and other non-operating items.

### Page 2

Unimicron Technology Corp (3037 TT) 
27 October 2026 
2

• Looking at the core business, 3Q26 revenue reached TWD52.7bn (up 23% QoQ, up 
55% YoY), with gross margin expanding 4.0ppt QoQ to 28.8% and operating margin 
rising 4.5ppt QoQ to 20.0%, supported by improving profitability in ABF substrates and 
HDI. With stronger operating leverage, operating profit surged 58% QoQ to 
TWD10.53bn. We believe the gross-margin and operating-margin performance was 
broadly in line with recent buy-side expectations for modestly improving profitability in 
ABF substrates and HDI, given that ABF margin was already high at c.40% in 2Q26, 
alongside a higher revenue contribution from a large end customer. We estimate ABF 
substrate margin at c.42-44% in 3Q26 (vs. c.40% in 2Q26).

• Business outlook. While the company does not provide numerical guidance, revenue 
and gross margin should continue to improve in 4Q26 amid the current substrate supply 
shortage. In addition, the company is aggressively expanding capacity at its Guangfu 
and Yangmei plants over 2027-29, with roughly c.30% annual ABF capacity expansion, 
which should become a key growth driver for Unimicron. Given Unimicron’s leading 
position across most CSP AI ASIC projects, Nvidia GPUs, and EMIB-T, we believe the 
company should continue to benefit from a favourable pricing environment amid the 
current supply-demand dynamics and ongoing specification upgrades, including larger 
substrate body sizes and technology migration.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Unimicron with a 12M TP of TWD1,500, based on a target 
PER of 35x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risk: less-than-
expected demand.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo, we raise our forecasts and TP 
for Win Semiconductors (WinSemi), but lower our rating by one notch to 
Outperform (2) on valuation grounds. While its topline trajectory appears to 
be unfolding intact, its margin trajectory is running better than our 
expectations. This, together with management’s positive commentary 
across its key business lines of RF, infrastructure and optics, reinforces our 
thesis that 3 growth pillars will turn around WinSemi’s bottom line and 
optimise its structural profitability (Upgrading: profit optimisation round the 
corner, 24 July). We suggest accumulating only on pullbacks.

What’s the impact: Forecast revisions. We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 
2-11% for WinSemi to factor in its higher margin trajectory, primarily on 
better product mix geared towards the infrastructure business, followed by 
the ramp-up in optics for AI optical communications (OC). We note the 
surge in our 2026E EPS revision is a result of the company’s non-operating 
gains of TWD684m in 3Q26, mainly from its 23%-owned ITEQ (6213 TT, 
TWD778, Buy [1], covered by Sheng Cheng), which distorts WinSemi’s 
growth trajectory at the bottom line into 2027.

Transformation paying off. At its results call, management commented on 
resilient RF demand from iOS and for WiFi routers, strong demand for low 
Earth orbit (LEO) satellite communications, and a continued ramp in pin 
diode (PD) sales after the product entered mass-production from late-
2Q26. This should be followed by laser diode (LD), such as continuous 
wave (CW) and electro-absorption modulated laser (EML), though 
customer qualifications will take time. All the commentary reinforces our 
thesis that 3 growth pillars will help turn around WinSemi’s bottom line for 
profitability optimisation after its business transformation: 1) RF business 
transformation paying off with premiumisation across cellular and WiFi for 
differentiation, 2) high-margin infrastructure scaling up on a robust LEO 
build, and 3) AI OC ramping fast, with broadened offerings from peripheral 
chips (optical drivers and modulators) to core chips for PD and LD.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lift our 12M TP for 
WinSemi to TWD620 (previous: TWD449), based on a higher 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 55x (previous: 50x), justified by its 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR of 54% (previous: 50%) from a PEG perspective. Yet we lower our 
rating by one notch to Outperform (2) on valuation grounds, with key 
downside risk of commodity cost hikes worsening consumer demand.

How we differ: Our 2026E EPS is 40% above consensus due to our 
inclusion of the non-operating gains in 3Q26, while our 2027E EPS is 9% 
above likely on our higher margin assumptions.

29 October 2026

Downgrading: profit optimisation on the go

➢ Raising our forecasts on a higher margin trajectory 
➢ Growth pillars paying off across infrastructure, optics and RF 
➢ Downgrading our rating to Outperform (2), with a higher TP of TWD620

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Win Semiconductors Corp (WinSemi) is the world’s largest compound semiconductor 
foundry, focusing on gallium-arsenide (GaAs) foundry services for customers in both wireless and wire-
line communication markets, as well as infrastructure applications. It has a diverse technology portfolio 
of processes that supports microwave frequency requirements from 50MHz to 100GHz, with 
applications including smartphones, tablet PCs, cellular base-stations, very small aperture terminals 
(VSAT) and fibre optics. It has started penetrating into vertical cavity surface emitting laser (VCSEL) 
foundries since 2017 for consumer laser applications such as 3D laser sensors and cameras.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 4 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
WinSemi's board of directors (BoD) has 10 members, including 3 independent directors,
representing 30% of total directors. Among the BoD, three of its non-independent directors are
also executive management.
Capital management 2
Since 2017, its dividend payout ratio has remained above 58%, except for 2024 when no
dividend was paid. Despite being the largest compound semiconductor foundry in the world,
WinSemi is continually expanding its capacity for future growth. Thus, we see the balance
between investment and dividend as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1 It did not have any meaningful related-party transactions in 2023.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
WinSemi We actively cooperate with local suppliers and improve their development capabilities,
reduce carbon emissions and create local employment opportunities. WinSemi also conducts on-
site audits of suppliers and annual assessment of suppliers on environmental protection and
safety & health management systems. In 2023, 100% of WinSemi's new suppliers signed the
"WIN Supplier Code of Conduct".'"
E Water & wastewatermanagement 1
Effluent that flows out of WinSemi’s facilities will first undergo pre-treatment in-house and then
treatment by the wastewater treatment plant at Hwaya Technology Park before discharge. The
plant has an acid and alkali wastewater recycling system and chemical mechanical polishing
wastewater recycling system for wastewater management.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
WinSemi has established a hazardous substance-free management procedure and also
collaborates with its suppliers to produce products that are hazardous substance-free for
customers and consumers. WinSemi specifies in purchase contracts and order forms with its
suppliers that the provided materials may not contain hazardous substances as defined by
WinSemi.
For waste management, it aims to increase the recycling rate of waste solvents and reduce the
use of raw materials in production.
E GHG emissions 1
For reduction of energy consumption, it prioritises high-efficiency and low energy consumption
models when replacing equipment and has also established annual energy conservation
management objectives. WinSemi targets to reduce electricity consumption (kWh/6"e wafer) by
12% from 2018 to 458.58 and PFCs emission (ton CO2e/6"e wafer) by 50% from 2018 to 0.114
in 2030.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

• Hon Hai's partnership with NVIDIA is extending across GPU, CPUs, LPU, 
DPUs, networking and integrated compute systems. Management 
highlighted more than ten dedicated manufacturing facilities supporting 
Vera Rubin production, underscoring the scale of its preparation. A notable 
engineering breakthrough is the redesign of compute trays to eliminate 
conventional cables, liquid-cooling hoses and fans, simplifying assembly, 
improving reliability and enabling greater automation. Hon Hai also 
showcased its work on robotic assembly, leveraging Omniverse digital 
twins, computer vision and physical AI models to automate complex 
operations previously requiring manual precision. These include busbar 
installation and high-density connector insertion. We believe deeper 
design-for-manufacturing and design-for-automation capabilities should 
help Hon Hai accelerate production ramps, improve manufacturing yields 
and as AI systems become increasingly complex. We also believe this will be 
the core competence for Hon Hai to secure next-generation rack-scale 
systems, where manufacturing complexity and system-level validation 
increasingly differentiate suppliers.
• NVIDIA DSX POD, a factory-integrated, pre-validated modular AI 
infrastructure solution, provided by Hon Hai. Each POD contains seven 
Vera Rubin NVL72 and three Spectrum-6 SPX racks, and incorporating 
more than 10 million components. By completing rack integration, cabling, 
liquid-cooling connections and functional testing before shipment, the 
solution aims to reduce onsite deployment from approximately six months 
to as little as 14 days. NVIDIA expects its partner ecosystem to deliver 
these solutions beginning in early 2027. Hon Hai is also collaborating with

TECO on modular data centers that integrate IT systems, power distribution and cooling within 40-foot containers,
with more than 80% of modular assembly completed at the factory. We believe this model could meaningfully
expand Hon Hai’s revenue opportunity beyond rack assembly into fully integrated infrastructure, while accelerating
time to first revenue-generating AI tokens.
• Hon Hai highlighted its expanding role in next-generation AI networking, including NVIDIA’s Spectrum-6 SN6800

CPO switch, delivering 409.6Tbps aggregate bandwidth through 512 ports at 800Gbps. Hon Hai showcased
capabilities spanning CPO optical components, ELSFP modules, high-speed connectivity and complete switch-tray
integration, positioning itself to capture additional networking content as optical interconnect adoption accelerates.
Next, we expect the scale-up CPO with NVL576 to be announced with VRU next year; Hon Hai will remain the sole
supplier, and in our view, its first-mover advantage will be supported for further revenue and earnings upside.

• Other than VR200 system, Hon Hai also demonstrated Nvidia's LPUs, integrated into its Vera Rubin ecosystem
through LPX inference racks containing 256 LPUs. Unlike GPUs optimized for parallel computation, LPUs
accelerate latency-sensitive token generation, particularly feed-forward and mixture-of-experts execution,
complementing Rubin GPUs handling prefill and attention workloads. We believe the heterogeneous GPU-LPU
computing could expand Hon Hai's addressable market in AI infrastructure integration.

• Beyond AI Data Centers: Hon Hai highlighted progress in EV, autonomous mobility, robotics and smart healthcare.  In
EVs, it introduced the affordable Model A multi-purpose vehicle, targeting Japan and Southeast Asia. Its CDMS
strategy focuses on shortening development cycles and improving cost competitiveness. In robotics, Hon Hai
demonstrated physical AI, humanoid robots and digital-twin-enabled manufacturing automation, supported by its
collaboration with NVIDIA and ABB Robotics.  The company also showcased emerging capabilities in SiC

semiconductors, low-Earth-orbit satellites and FoxBrain AI models. We believe these initiatives reinforce Hon Hai's
longer-term transformation into a diversified technology manufacturing platform.

Hon Hai Precision
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 18x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is at the higher end of the historical trading range.
We believe this multiple is justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook, as well as new
endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus

we believe a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand;
2) intensified peer competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress; and 4) higher-than-expected
impact of COVID-19 or other macro factors.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
Prepared for Kevin Lu

investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.

Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

ab 30 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Lite-On Technology
Cloud growth and margins stay healthy amid
supply chain constraints
Cloud growth reaffirmed; stronger margins cushion platform delays
Lite-On hosted its investor conference after the close Friday 10/30 and reaffirmed its full
year +70% cloud growth guidance. Sales were already reported slightly light at NT
$57.4bn, +8.9% slightly below UBS/Street +10.5%/14.3% estimates due to hardware

kitting issues and new platform delays. We continue to see strong growth with an
expanding customer base and strong ramp of compute but trim 2026/27E sales growth
from +31%/38% to +30%/35% to factor in component constraints and 800V
DC sidecar ramp more into 2028E due to engineering readiness. With the company
increasing its AI mix to 30% in 2026 and >40% in 2027 and ultimately ramping
HVDC in 2028, we expect it to sustain its current margin level, and maintain our Buy

rating with an unchanged NT$400 PT based on unchanged NT$20 2028E EPS.
Q3 growth constrained; margins expand on improving mix
Lite-On Q3 sales of  NT$57.4bn were slightly light with  Cloud/AIoT +57% YoY to 59% of
group revenue with growth capped by the components shortage, Opto-electro +17%
YoY (14% of sales) and  IT/CE -6% YoY (27% of sales) softer due to the weakening
consumer market more than offsetting the  strong SpaceX LEO power supply growth.

The group GM/OpM was better at 25.9%/15.1% vs. UBSe/Street 15.2%/13.8% on
better mix and operating leverage to lift Q3 EPS to NT$3.27 vs. UBSe/Street NT
$2.85/2.97. The company exited Q3 with NT$66bn inventory, +20% QoQ/119% YoY
due to the components mismatch  and buffer inventory build-ups with LTAs.
Multiple growth drivers to sustain the growth in 2027 before HVDC ramps
Although the industry supply constraints might push the 18.3kW power shelves toward

end of Q4, we are seeing the 8.5kW PSU and BBU for AMD servers and ASICs
supporting the cloud growth in 2026. With more capacity to come online in H127, we
are seeing the 50V power rack to start shipping with BBU to ramp more than 30% YoY
in 2027. The company is increasing its 2026 capex from NT$19.7bn vs. prior NT$18bn to
meet the substantial cloud demand and expecting even larger spending in 2027, to
double their cloud capacity by the end of 2027.

Reiterate Buy rating with unchanged PT of NT$400
Lite-On's shares have corrected 24% from their August peak due to the supply chain
concern on platforms delay and component constraints. We trim our 2026-27 sales
growth estimates from +31.3%/37.5% to +29.6%/34.8% while expecting it to
reaccelerate with the 800VDC power rack ramp in 2028E to support +32% growth. We
expect margins to stay on the growth trend and fine-tune our 2026-27 EPS
estimates from  NT$10.85/NT$16.00 to NT$11.23/NT$15.00 and 2028E EPS unchanged

at NT$20.00 with NT$400 PT based on an unchanged 20x 2028E EPS.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Lite-On Technology using PE methodology. Key risks to our investment thesis:
The principal risk is a slower AI infrastructure deployment cycle. Our industry work shows
deployed IT GW is the single-largest determinant of the power TAM, so a meaningful

reduction in hyperscaler accelerator deployment would affect all power suppliers.
Customer concentration is a second risk, particularly Lite-On’s large custom-ASIC exposure
and reliance on a relatively small number of hyperscalers. Program delays or share changes
can materially affect annual revenue.
Execution on capacity expansion also matters. Lite-On is investing substantially ahead of
forecast demand; if utilization ramps more slowly, depreciation and fixed operating costs

could limit expected margin improvement.
Competitive pricing remains a risk as more suppliers qualify for high-power products. We
believe the move toward larger, more engineered modules and systems should moderate
pure commodity pricing pressure, but it will not eliminate it.
Architecture is another source of uncertainty. A slower transition to external power racks is
not particularly damaging to our near-term thesis because 48/54V systems still require

substantial power content. Longer term, however, the precise mix between rack-level BBUs,
pooled UPS/BESS, ±400V, single-bus 800V and facility-level conversion will determine where
value accrues within the supply chain.
Rising competition and slower spec upgrades would impact earnings. Faster progress from
Chinese power suppliers, FX movements and weaker legacy IT/consumer demand could
create additional earnings variability.
HOLT methodology
The HOLT methodology does not assign ratings or a target price to a security. It is an analytical

tool that involves use of a set of proprietary quantitative algorithms and warranted value
calculations, collectively called the HOLT valuation model, that are consistently applied to all
the companies included in its database. The HOLT valuation model is a discounted cash flow
model. Third-party data (including consensus earnings estimates) are systematically translated
into a number of default variables and incorporated into the algorithms available in the HOLT

valuation model. The source financial statement, pricing, and earnings data provided by
outside data vendors are subject to quality control and may also be adjusted to more closely
measure the underlying economics of firm performance. These adjustments provide
consistency when analyzing a single company across time, or analyzing multiple companies
across industries or national borders. The default scenario that is produced by the HOLT
valuation model establishes a warranted price that represents the expected mean value for a

security based upon empirically derived fade algorithms that forecast a firm's future return on
capital and growth rates over an extended period of time. As the third-party data are
updated, the warranted price updates automatically. A company’s future achieved return on
capital or growth rate may differ from HOLT default forecast. Additional information about
the HOLT methodology is available upon request.
CFROI, CFROE, HOLT, HOLT Lens, HOLTfolio and “Powered by HOLT” are trademarks or

The HOLT Business Resilience Dashboard (HBRD) is constructed by applying systematic
processes on publicly available records and third-party data to generate quantitative
measures. The HBRD consistently assigns scores using a transparent combination of metrics
and weights across financial, environmental and governance inputs. Individual companies
can be scored on a global-size and/or region-size relative basis. The HBRD is also translated
into five textual assessments (e.g. “Highest” or “Lowest”) based on their quintile percentile

rank. For further details on the HBRD methodology HOLT Business Resilience Primer please
contact UBS Securities LLC, 11 Madison Avenue, New York, NY 10010, USA, Attention:
Investment Research. The HBRD does not constitute or imply any endorsement or
recommendation of any individual company. UBS has not sought to independently verify
information obtained from public and third-party sources and makes no representations or
warranties as to accuracy, completeness or reliability of such information due to inconsistent

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 2

New capacity in expansion: The company continues to expand its production scale to 
fulfill rising clients’ orders from LEO satellite and innovative business. We expect to see 
growing LEO satellite revenues contribution from 75% in 2025 to 97% in 2028E. 
Management notes the company has started the planning of next-stage expansion, and 
will start to expand capacity in Taiwan, Vietnam and US sites from 4Q26E and onwards 
to diversify the global production sites, improve the product delivery capabilities, and 
supply elasticity.

Earnings revision: We factor in UMT 3Q26 earnings and revise down earnings by 5%/ 
10%/ 9% in 2026-28E mainly on lower GM in 2026-28E by 3.8ppts~3.9ppts, reflecting 
longer time of transition to new LEO satellites platform, while we expect to see 
continuous GM improvement ahead on rising LEO satellite buisness carrying higher GM.

Valuation: We continue to derive our target price based on a discounted P/E, and based 
on 2029E (methodology unchanged). We continue to derive the target P/E multiple from 
the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the satellite supply chain, and we remove outliers 
(telecom operators who face competition from LEO satellite operators). Our target 
2029E discounted P/E multiple is at 35.1x (vs. previously at 37.1x) which is based on 
peers’ avg. PEG&M, and UMT’s 2029-30E avg. NI YoY and OPM, due to lower avg. OPM in 
2029-30E at 38% (vs. previously at 42%). We apply the 35.1x multiple to 2029E EPS and

### Page 4

Price target risks and methodology - UMT 
Valuation: We use a discounted P/E methodology and apply a 35.1x target P/E multiple 
to UMT’s 2029E EPS, discounting it back to 2027E at a COE of 7.8% (beta 1.2x, risk-free 
rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), which leads to our 12-month target price 
of NT$2,188. The target P/E is based on the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the 
satellite supply chain. We are Buy-rated on UMT.  
Key Risks: Slower-than-expected LEO satellite deployment; Potential competition from 
new-entrant suppliers; LEO satellite operators manufacturing in-house components.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 30 Oct 2026 close.

Document 187: 35岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了

Source PDF: all report pdf/35岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

最缺的不是更强的  AI 模型，⽽是既懂业务、⼜能把  AI 真正⽤起来的⼈。
摘要：
今年以来，全球科技公司已宣布裁员近  20 万⼈。与此同时，有⼀个  AI 岗位的招聘需求却在爆
发：美国的招聘⼴告三年涨了  30 多倍，薪资中位数约  19.5 万美元；国内相关职位的发布量⼀
年涨了  21 倍。
OpenAI 、 Anthropic 、微软、亚⻢逊、⾕歌，⼏家巨头前后砸下近百亿美元，有的开新公司，有
的建新部⻔，抢的都是这类⼈才。最反常识的是：这个岗位最稀缺的本事，恰恰不是写代码。
Anthropic ：培养  1 万个 “ 把  AI 送进企业的⼈ ”
10 ⽉  2 ⽇， Claude 的开发商  Anthropic 宣布投⼊  1 亿美元成⽴ “Claude 前沿学院 ” （ Claude
Frontier Academy ），⽬标是在  2027 年底前培养  1 万名 “ 前沿部署⼯程师 ” 。
⾸批学员是从合作伙伴中选出的在职⼯程师，来⾃埃森哲、摩根⼠丹利、诺和诺德和⼏家顶级
咨询公司。培训分两段：先是  4 天线下集训，从⼀次 “ 模拟企业部署 ” 开始；考核通过后，学员回
到⾃⼰公司，带⼀个真实项⽬做  12 周实战。⾸批认证预计在  2027 年初完成。
在  Anthropic 看来，企业落地  AI 的瓶颈不在模型，⽽在于缺少能推动变⾰的⼈。负责这个项⽬
的⾼管  Steve Corfield 说，客⼾现在缺的，是既懂业务怎么运转、⼜能亲⼿把  AI 从⼀个想法做
成上线系统的⼈。
这只是今年  AI 巨头抢⼈⼤战中的最新⼀例。

砸下百亿美元，巨头们都在抢同⼀种⼈才
今年  5 ⽉， OpenAI 成⽴了专⻔帮企业落地  AI 的 “OpenAI 部署公司 ” ，由  TPG 领投，⾸轮融资
超过  40 亿美元。同⽉， Anthropic 与⿊⽯、 Hellman & Friedman 、⾼盛合资成⽴了⼀家  AI 服
务公司，各⽅初始投资约  15 亿美元。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

6 ⽉底到  7 ⽉初，亚⻢逊  AWS 和微软先后出⼿： AWS 投⼊  10 亿美元，组建⾃⼰的部署⼯程
团队；微软投⼊  25 亿美元成⽴新部⻔，把约  6000 名⼯程专家派驻到客⼾企业。 9 ⽉，⾕歌云
和埃森哲成⽴联合业务部⻔，计划培训最多  1000 名埃森哲⼯程师，专⻔派驻客⼾现场。
过去这些公司⽐的是谁的模型更强、参数更⼤、跑分更⾼，如今却不约⽽同地把钱砸向同⼀个
⽅向：派⼈进客⼾公司，把  AI 真正⽤起来。
这类⼈才在业内有个统⼀的叫法：FDE （ Forward Deployed Engineer ），中⽂常译作“ 前沿部
署⼯程师 ”。
招聘量三年猛涨 30 倍，⼤⼚开出百万年包
熟悉美股的读者，对这个词的源头不会陌⽣。⼗多年前， Palantir （帕兰泰尔）就系统化地推⾏
了这种做法：⼯程师不坐在总部写通⽤产品，⽽是常驻客⼾现场，和客⼾⽤同⼀套数据、扛同
⼀个业务指标，把模糊的需求、分散的数据⼀点点变成能⽤的系统。 Palantir CEO Alex Karp 有

⼀个被业内反复引⽤的判断：⼤模型当然重要，但⾄少在未来⼏年，价值在于落地实施。
如今，这套做法正在变成⼀个热⻔职业。据劳动⼒市场分析机构  Lightcast 统计， 2023 年全美
FDE 招聘⼴告不到  200 条， 2026 年前九个⽉已超过  7300 条，三年涨了  30 多倍。⼴告标出的
薪资中位数约  19.5 万美元，是全美招聘⼴告薪资中位数的三倍多。发布  FDE ⼴告最多的雇主
中，德勤、埃森哲等咨询公司排在⾕歌等科技巨头之前。可⻅  FDE 早已不只是  AI 公司的技术
岗，凡是要把  AI ⽤进业务的地⽅，都需要这样的⼈。
FDE 这股⻛已经刮到国内。脉脉平台数据显⽰， 2026 年上半年，平台上  FDE 相关职位的发布
量同⽐飙升  21 倍。字节跳动的 “ ⾖包  AI ⼤模型  FDE” 年包最⾼约  105 万元，蚂蚁数科、智谱等
也开出了  90 万元以上的年包。但即便开出⾼薪，合适的⼈依然难招：猎聘⼤数据研究院统计，
2025 年  9 ⽉⾄  2026 年  8 ⽉，国内  FDE ⼈才的供需⽐只有  0.28 ，相当于将近  4 个岗位抢  1 个

⼈。
这个缺⼝还在被政策放⼤。⼯信部  8 ⽉底印发通知，⿎励  AI 应⽤服务商搭建  FDE 团队、扎根
⽤⼾现场；武汉光⾕提出  3 年培育  1000 名  FDE ，上海提出要建⽴超千⼈规模的  FDE ⼯程师储
备库。企业、服务商、地⽅政府都在要⼈，合格的  FDE 远远不够⽤。
AI 落地难在业务，最缺的恰恰是懂业务的⼈
看到这⾥，很多⼈会想：这是年轻程序员的机会，我不写代码，也过了转⾏的年纪。但不妨看
看  Anthropic 怎么描述⼀个好的  FDE ：熟悉  Agent 系统、擅⻓解决问题、善于沟通、有⼯程经
验。这四条⾥，有⼀半在技术之外。
AI 落地最难的部分，本来就不在代码。过去两年，⼏乎每家企业都买过  AI 产品、做过试点，真
正跑进⽇常业务的却少得多。标普全球的调查显⽰，放弃⼤部分  AI 项⽬的企业，⼀年内从  17%
涨到了  42% ； IDC 和联想  2025 年的报告更直接： 88% 的  AI 概念验证项⽬，最后都没能投⼊
⽣产。
演⽰时⼀切顺利，⼀进真实业务，就撞上脏数据、⽼系统、权限审批、员⼯习惯，还有那个绕

不开的问题：出了错谁负责。这也是  FDE 和驻场外包的区别：外包是甲⽅说什么就做什么；
FDE 要先判断这件事该不该⽤  AI 、⽤哪个模型、花多少钱、怎么证明有效，再动⼿上线。
所以  FDE 真正值钱的那⼀半能⼒，是懂业务。⼀份合同哪⼏条要紧，⼀张报表哪个数字会出
事，⼀个审批流程卡在哪⼀步，哪件事交给机器会让⽼板睡不着觉，这些  AI 替代不了，在⾏业
⾥⼲了⼗⼏、⼆⼗年的⼈反⽽最清楚。

### Page 3

腾讯云副总裁张渝前不久的⼀句话，戳中了很多企业的痛处：过去花⼤价钱请外部团队做项
⽬， " 项⽬做完，团队撤场，能⼒也跟着带⾛了 " 。越来越多企业意识到，与其等外部团队来了⼜
⾛，不如让⾃⼰懂业务的⼈⻓出这项能⼒。
如果你已经在⾏业⾥深耕多年，那么你缺的从来不是经验，⽽是把经验交给  AI 去执⾏的那⼀
步：知道今天的  AI 能做什么、做不了什么，该怎么搭、要花多少钱、出了错怎么兜底。我们这
次的训练营，就是为这⼀步准备的。
两天⾛完  AI 落地最后⼀公⾥
华尔街⻅闻⾸期「 FDE 实战训练营」将于  11 ⽉  14 ⽇⾄  15 ⽇在上海开班。这是⼀个线下实战
营，限量  50 席，在两天时间⾥，带你亲⼿搭出⾃⼰的第⼀个  AI Agent ，⾛完⼀遍从选模型、算
成本到评测上线的全流程。
先说说  Agent （智能体）是什么。很多⼈对  AI 的印象还停留在 “ 问⼀句答⼀句 ” 的聊天上，但
Agent 不⼀样：你交给它⼀件事，它会⾃⼰查资料、打开软件、读写⽂件、交出结果，做错了还
能⾃⼰返⼯。巨头们派  FDE 进企业，部署的正是这种技术。
两天训练营共  12 个模块，从模型怎么选、价格怎么算，⼀直讲到评测、成本优化和安全上线，
都是⼤⼚把  AI 做上线时真正在⽤的⽅法。主讲讲师  Right 曾任腾讯⾼级算法⼯程师，主导过微
信核⼼  AI Agent 项⽬的算法设计和规模化落地，在  Agent 系统设计、模型训练、 Harness 架构
⽅向有深厚积累。他还在  NeurIPS 、 ACL 等顶级会议发表过多篇  Agent ⽅向论⽂，相关⼯作被
Google DeepMind 引⽤。
课程适合谁
企业主和管理者：想弄清  AI 到底能替公司做哪些事、每件事花多少钱、⻛险在哪⾥
⾦融、法务、财务、运营、咨询等专业⼈⼠：⼿上有⼤量重复⼯作，想亲⼿做⼀个⼯具交给
AI
想在  AI 时代给⾃⼰多留⼀项硬技能的⼈

三⼗年前，先学会⽤  Excel 的那批⼈，在后来的⼆⼗年⾥少熬了⽆数个夜。
今天巨头们花百亿美元在抢懂业务、⼜会⽤  AI 的⼈，⽽你已经有了⼀半。剩下那⼀半，就在这
两天⾥补⻬。

【大盤指數/股價】46096 / 254.5

➢ 3Q26 毛利率回升，獲利大致符合預期 
3Q26 營收 253.34 億元(QoQ+9.6%)，成長來源中邏輯(非記憶體)與記
憶體各約一半；記憶體增加約 11 億元，主要來自 DRAM；YoY 成長中
NAND Flash 增加超過 20 億元 ，3Q26 毛利率 24%， 較前季增加2.2ppts
主要來自於產能利用率高檔以及價格調升，營業外利益 0.85 億元，稅後
獲利 26.12 億元，QoQ+17.7%，YoY+69.9%，EPS 3.44 元略低於我們
預估的 3.50 元。其中 3Q26 封裝、測試產能利用率皆超越 90%，後續
將藉由淘汰老舊機台維持在接近滿載水準， 4Q26 營收及毛利率持續改
善，3Q26 毛利率提升主要來自於整體產品毛利率結構改善(產品組合影
響不大)。 
➢ 4Q26 受惠於漲價趨勢，2026 年營運大幅改善 
以目前市況來看，MU 仍持續擴大 DRAM 委外訂單(Mobile、graphics 
DRAM、LPDDR5)，NAND 景氣回溫需求明顯，國票預估力成 4Q26
營收 264.32 億元(QoQ＋4.33%)，毛利率因價格調整上升到 24.2%，較
前一季增加 0.2ppts，Logic 產品也轉往高毛利為主 ， 淨利為32.68 億元 ，
QoQ＋25.1%，YoY+75.4%，EPS 4.31 元，在 DRAM/Flash 景氣仍處
在高檔的狀況下，子公司超豐也往高附加價值的 FCBGA 為主，Tera 
Probe 以高階測試為主，高階記憶體/LTA 比重持續上揚，也將帶動力成
營收及毛利率進一步放大，投顧預估力成 2026 年營收將達 961.96 億
元，EPS 13.10 元。 
➢ 2027 年展望持續樂觀 
AI Chip、OE 產品/CPO/TSV interconnection DRAM 目前驗證進度順
利，量產計畫維持不變，目標成為全球第一家量產 FOPLP AI 應用的公
司、與Broadcom 共同開發及生產用於先進封裝基板上的加成式細線寬
重佈層封裝技術順利，我們預估力成在整體 FOPLP/記憶體市況良好的
狀況下 ，2027 年營收為 1,139.7 億元(YoY+18.48%)，EPS 可以達到 17.97
元優異水準。 
➢ 維持買進評等，目標價 306 元 
公司為封測大廠，先進封測營收占比超越 30%，獲利能力大幅度改善，
維持正向看法 ， 過去本益比區間位於6X~20X， 目標價306 元(2027F PE 
17X)。

### Page 2

個股報告

3Q26 毛利率回升，獲利大致符合預期 
3Q26 營收 253.34 億元(QoQ+9.6%)， 成長來源中邏輯(非記憶體)與記憶體各約一半 ；
記憶體增加約 11 億元，主要來自 DRAM；YoY 成長中 NAND Flash 增加超過 20 億
元，3Q26 毛利率 24%，較前季增加 2.2ppts 主要來自於產能利用率高檔以及價格調
升，營業外利益 0.85 億元，稅後獲利 26.12 億元，QoQ+17.7%，YoY+69.9%，EPS 
3.44 元略低於我們預估的 3.50 元。其中 3Q26 封裝、測試產能利用率皆超越 90%，
後續將藉由淘汰老舊機台維持在接近滿載的水準 ，4Q26 營收及毛利率持續改善 ，3Q26
毛利率提升主要來自於整體產品毛利率結構改善(產品組合影響不大)。營收結構：封裝 
67%、 測試24%(約 60.8 億元)、SIP 與模組 9%； 產品別為DRAM 20%、NAND 28%、
SIP 9%、 邏輯43%。其中 NAND 出貨仍以 eSSD 為主不管是價跟量都有顯著的提升。
超豐受惠於客戶需求強勁、產品價格調整及簽訂長約(LTA)，帶動營收雙位數成長。晶
兆成(TeraPower)與 TeraPro 因 AI 測試需求暢旺，擴大資本支出滿足客戶，營收展現
高雙位數成長。 
4Q26 受惠於漲價趨勢，2026 年營運大幅改善 
以目前市況來看， MU 仍持續擴大 DRAM 委外訂單 (Mobile、graphics DRAM 、
LPDDR5)，NAND 景氣回溫需求明顯，國票預估力成 4Q26 營收 264.32 億元(QoQ
＋4.33%)，毛利率因價格調整上升到 24.2%，較前一季增加 0.2ppts，Logic 產品也轉
往高毛利為主，淨利為 32.68 億元，QoQ＋25.1%，YoY+75.4%，EPS 4.31 元，在
DRAM/Flash 景氣仍處在高檔的狀況下 ， 子公司超豐也往高附加價值的FCBGA 為主，
Tera Probe 以高階測試為主，高階記憶體/LTA 比重持續上揚，也將帶動力成營收及毛
利率進一步放大，DRAM：訂單能見度高、供不應求，產能利用率可望維持相當水準；
客戶新產能陸續開出為後續觀察點 ，NAND／SSD： 企業級SSD 與 NAND 需求強勁，
第四季整體需求維持高檔；封裝材料成本上升已適度反映於客戶價格，對第四季營收
與毛利率有正面支撐。AI 需求帶動高階 FCBGA 產能需求擴大，第四季高階封裝業務
延續成長；原物料上漲帶動的價格調整已獲客戶接受，預期進一步推升營收與獲利。
投顧預估力成 2026 年營收將達 961.96 億元，EPS 13.10 元。 
2027 年展望持續樂觀 
AI Chip 、OE 產品/CPO/TSV interconnection DRAM 目前驗證進度順利，量產計畫
維持不變，目標成為全球第一家量產 FOPLP AI 應用的公司、與 Broadcom 共同開發
及生產用於先進封裝基板上的加成式細線寬重佈層封裝技術順利， 力成堅持採用  
510mm×515mm 面板尺寸 ， 相較於310mm×310mm 具備更高面積使用率與產出效
益，且技術門檻優於 600mm×600mm。第三季成功量產大尺寸(約 75mm×78mm)
晶片封裝 ， 第四季將進一步跨入更大尺寸(如 78mm×88mm)整合 SoC 與 DRAM 之異
質封裝我們預估力成在整體 FOPLP/記憶體市況良好的狀況下 ，2027 年營收為 1,139.7
億元(YoY+18.4%)，EPS 可以達到 17.97 元優異水準。 
維持買進評等，目標價 306 元 
公司為封測大廠，先進封裝相關業務(Fan-out BGA 產品、Fan-Out POP 、TSV 等)已
佔集團營收三成以上。而目前與 AMD 合資 FOPLP 將於 2027 年小量貢獻營收，記憶
體市場 2027 年整體供需結構不變有利於封測價格的調整，使得力成獲利不再受到原
物料的衝擊，獲利能力大幅度改善，維持正向看法，過去本益比區間位於 6X~20X，
目標價 306 元(2027F PE 17X)。

強力買進/買進 93

評等 定義 
強力買進 預估未來 3 個月內的絕對報酬高於 40%，且短期股價動能強勁 
買    進 
• 預估未來 3 個月內的絕對報酬高於 20% 
• 中大型股及金融股：預估未來 3 個月內的絕對報酬高於
12%以上 
區間操作 
• 預估未來 3 個月內絕對報酬小於 20% 
• 中大型股及金融股：預估未來 3 個月內的絕對報酬小於
12% 
觀    望 預估未來 3 個月內的絕對報酬低於 0% 
未 評 等 • 該公司未於國票核心持股名單 • 國票可能與該公司進行業務而處於緘默期

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 여전히 강력하다SK하이닉스(000660) 여전히 강력하다여전히 강력하다 - 기업.pdf

Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 8

### Page 1

00

SK 하이닉스 (000660/KS) 
여전히 강력하다 
3Q26 영업이익 83 조원으로 예상 상회 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 83조원 (+38% QoQ)으로 예상을 상회했다. 메모
리 공급 부족 심화, HBM4 반영 시작에 따른 메모리 가격 상승이 환율 하락을 상쇄했
기 때문이다. 출하 DRAM +10%, NAND -1%, 가격 DRAM +28%, NAND 
+24%, 영업이익률 DRAM 82%, NAND 72%, HBM 판가는 HBM4 출하가 시작되
며 37% 수준 상승한 것으로 추정된다. 업황은 여전히 예상을 상회하고 있다.

4Q26 분기 영업이익 100 조원 시대 진입 전망 
4Q26 영업이익은 105조원 (+27% QoQ)으로 분기 100조원 시대에 진입할 것으로 
전망한다. 메모리 가격 강세가 지속되는 가운데, 1c 출하 가속화, M15X 램프업 본격화 
등으로 출하량 상승이 기존 예상을 상회할 것이다. 또한 HBM4의 Cross-over 시작 
및 수율 안정화로 HBM 가격, 수익성 제고 동반이 지속될 것이다. 출하는 DRAM 
+24%, NAND +4%, 가격은 DRAM +8%, NAND +7%를 예상한다.

2027 년 영업이익 488 조원 (+70% YoY)으로 상향조정 
SK하이닉스의 2027년 영업이익을 488조원 (+70% YoY)으로 12% 상향 조정한
다. 원/달러 환율 전망치를 기존 1,340원에서 1,315원으로 하향 조정했음에도, 공급 
부족 심화 속 2027년향 HBM 가격 인상 및 예상을 상회하는 수율, 1Q27 진행될 모바
일 DRAM 가격 인상 등을 반영했기 때문이다. LTA 가격 역시 개별 약정된 범위 내 
시장 상황을 반영할 수 있는 구조라는 점을 감안하면, 지속 상승할 것이라 전망한다.

높은 주주환원 의지 확인, 높아진 실적 가시성, 지나친 저평가 
투자의견 매수, 목표주가 4,000,000원을 유지한다. SK하이닉스는 LTA에 대해 기
존 5년에서 추가 연장에 대한 논의가 진행 중이며, 2029년까지 HBM, DRAM은 사실
상 완판, HBM 수요는 공급 가능 물량의 2 배를 상회, 2030년까지 메모리 수요 우위 
국면 전망을 밝혔다. AI 인프라 투자의 강력한 명분과 메모리 수요의 구조성, 잠재적으
로 매우 높은 LTA 위반 비용을 감안하면 실적 가시성과 안정성 제고가 동반되는 국면
이다. 100조원 수준의 주주환원은 2026년 예상 FCF의 66% 수준, 분기별 자사주 
매입, 소각 지속 계획을 통해 높은 주주환원 의지를 보여주고 있다. 실적 Duration 장
기화, 주주환원을 감안하면 12MF P/E 3.4X 수준의 현 주가는 현저한 저평가 국면이
다. 과도한 우려의 되돌림과 메모리 밸류에 대한 재논의 국면 진입을 전망한다.

2026-10-29

반도체. 한동희 / donghee.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-8826

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다.

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%) 과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%) 을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

### Page 1

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 건설/원전 | 2026. 10. 30  
숫자로 증명하는 중

3Q26 Review: 에너빌부문 믹스개선, 두산밥캣 호실적 
두산에너빌리티 3분기 매출액은 4조 4,494억원으로 전년동기대비 14.7% 증가, 컨센
서스에 부합했다. 영업이익은 2,612억원으로 전년동기대비 90.6% 증가, 컨센서스 영
업이익 2,464억원 대비 6.0% 상회하는 호실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티부문의 원자
력, 가스터빈 매출 증가에 따른 믹스 개선 효과와 두산밥캣의 가격정책 효과에 힘입은 
마진 개선으로 안정적인 실적 기조가 이어지고 있어 긍정적이다. 분기 환평가손실로 
인해 순이익은 여타 수출기업과 유사하게 컨센서스 대비 하회했다. 에너빌부문의 수주
는 누계 10조원으로 목표대비 75%를 채웠으며, 수주 대기중인 안건들을 고려할 때 
당분간 수주 업사이클이 몇년간 지속될 것임이 분명한 시점이다. 
기회가 아닌 경쟁력, 모멘텀이 아닌 수주  
두산에너빌리티에 대해 투자의견 Buy 유지하나, 목표주가를 95,000원으로 하향했다. 
목표 EBITDA는 2035년 EBITDA에 WACC 8%를 적용, 목표 EV/EBITDA는 Global 
Peers인 GE Vernova의 2026년 EV/EBITDA 멀티플에서 자회사 실적 변동성을 고
려해 10% 할인 적용했다. 호실적과 수주 증가에도 GEV의 멀티플 감소와 순차입금 
증가로 인한 NAV 감소를 밸류에이션에 반영한 결과이다. 전일 주가는 한수원의 체코 
원전 시공사 협상 결렬로 인해 7.65% 하락 마감했다. 시공 수주는 아쉽지만 두산에너
빌리티의 체코 원전 기자재 납품에는 전혀 영향이 없으며, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가로 경
제적해자인 대형 가스 터빈 수급 쇼티지는 당분간 지속될 전망임을 고려할 때 전일 주
가는 낙폭 과대로 판단한다. 1) 두산에너빌리티의 가스터빈은 이미 2031년 출하 물량
에 대한 예약 주문이 마무리되고 있으며, 2) 또한 AP1000의 우량한 수주 파이프라인
과, 3) 뉴스케일파워, 테라파워, 롤스로이스, X Energy 등의 SMR 제작을 위한 협업 역
시 지속되고 있다는 점에 주목한다. 전일 기사를 통해 뉴스케일파워 SMR 1호 제작 계
약을 체결했다는 소식을 전했으며, 이제 에너지 트렌지션은 기회가 아닌 회사의 경쟁
력으로, 가스터빈과 원전은 모멘텀이 아니라 수주로 입증되고 있다.  
 Analyst 김세련

Document 368: Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

Jan Hatzius 
+1(212)902-0394 | jan.hatzius@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC
Global Views: Downside Growth Risks
26 October 2026 | 6:45AM EDT
1. So far in 2026, global growth has been remarkably unremarkable.  Both the world 
as a whole and the G3 economies are growing in line with our estimates of potential 
GDP .  Our interpretation is that fiscal easing and the positive impulse from the AI 
boom have offset the negative impulse from the war in Iran and the lingering drag 
from higher US tariffs.

2. Our standing global GDP forecasts project more of the same in 2027, as lower 
energy prices offset fading tailwinds from fiscal policy and AI.  But the balance of 
risks is on the downside.  The most immediate concern is that, far from subsiding, 
energy prices have again climbed sharply on fears of renewed Middle East escalation.  
The prices of gasoline, diesel, jet fuel, and European/Asian natural gas are now near 
or even above their March/April highs, with likely negative effects on consumers and 
energy-importing economies more broadly.

### Page 2

3. Although we don’t expect significant fiscal policy changes in 2027, the consequences 
of large-scale deficits—coupled with AI-linked borrowing and central bank hikes—have 
started to show up more clearly in higher longer-term interest rates.  The moves have 
been mostly orderly, but the increase since early 2026 averages a sizable 100bp across 
DM yield curves.  If sustained, we estimate that this increase will subtract a cumulative 
0.6% from GDP via weakness in housing, consumer durables, and business investment.  
(Only part of the drag is incorporated in our standing forecasts because we expect bond 
yields to pull back as central banks deliver fewer hikes than priced in markets.)

Exhibit 2: Oil Price Risks Are Skewed to the Upside

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   2
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 3

4. The growth impulse from the AI boom was set to peak in late 2026 or early 2027 even 
before concerns around the sustainability of the AI investment boom intensified in 
recent weeks.  Although AI investment is still rising rapidly, we expect the increase as a 
share of GDP to slow over the next year.  Alongside the fading boost from last year’s 
increase in US depreciation allowances, this will weigh on capital spending growth, 
especially in the US.  AI has also boosted US consumer spending growth via a positive 
wealth effect worth an estimated 0.4pp (or 0.25% of GDP) over the past year.  This 
impact is likely to decline over the next year, gradually if stocks perform in line with our 
strategists’ reasonably upbeat expectations but more sharply if AI-related stocks 
continue to decline.

### Page 4

5. Our own base case remains that AI investment will ultimately pay off in terms of higher 
productivity and economywide profits.  However, one key channel for this payoff is labor 
market disruption, which has negative near-term macroeconomic effects.  And while DM 
labor markets still look solid in aggregate, we are starting to see clearer signs that the AI 
impact is spreading beyond the technology sector.  Across nonmanufacturing industries, 
the share of US firms that have adopted AI in regular business functions now shows a 
statistically significant negative correlation with employment growth and a statistically 
significant positive correlation with unemployment.

6. The Fed’s recent signals—explicit from Williams/Jefferson and implicit from Warsh—
effectively rule out a hike on October 28.  But the FOMC still looks very likely to deliver 
another 25bp hike to 4-4.25% on December 9 because spot inflation remains too high.  
And while we continue to expect significant disinflation next year on the back of 
reduced impulses from tariffs, energy prices, and AI-related prices, the risks to our 
forecast are on the upside, most obviously in energy.  Higher inflation could result in

### Page 5

another hike in March, but either way we see renewed cuts in late 2027/early 2028.

7. Like the Fed, the ECB will likely hold rates steady next week and deliver a final hike (to 
2.75%) at the December meeting.  However, the turbulence in the French bond market 
poses somewhat greater downside risks to that call than in the US.  The turbulence has 
tightened financial conditions to a degree that is likely to shave ¼-½pp from annualized 
growth in coming quarters.  And if it intensifies into December, markets might perceive a 
hike as signaling insufficient focus by the ECB on the risk of Euro area fragmentation.  
While the ECB could address this issue by coupling a hike with renewed balance sheet 
expansion, the hurdle for such a move is high, in part because purchases of French 
bonds would inevitably raise concerns about moral hazard with respect to the needed 
fiscal adjustment.

8. Across the English Channel, we expect the MPC to raise Bank Rate by 25bp to 4% on

### Page 6

November 5.  Consistent with our view that the UK economy is stronger than widely 
perceived, GDP growth has surprised on the upside this year and the unemployment rate 
declined to 4.7% in August on a 3-month average basis (with the volatile 1-month rate 
plunging to 4.1%).  Rising inflation and the looming household energy price reset in 
January imply a significant risk of further moves, although market pricing of 4+ hikes 
(including November) still looks excessive.  We also expect hikes in Canada and New 
Zealand this week and in Australia next week.

9. Slightly faster than expected growth in Q3 has pushed up our 2026 GDP forecast for 
China to 4.6%.  But most activity indicators are much weaker.  Both property activity and 
fixed asset investment are contracting year-on-year, and while nominal retail sales 
growth picked up to +1.1% in September, large retailers (whose sales are relatively 
better measured) are still posting substantial year-on-year declines.  With domestic 
demand rising slowly at best, sustained GDP growth in the official 4.5-5% target range 
will require ongoing steep increases in the trade surplus.  But the surplus is already at 1% 
of global GDP , and further increases will eat more noticeably into output and 
employment abroad.  Ultimately, China’s trading partners may respond by imposing 
trade barriers that weigh on Chinese exports and GDP , although the measures taken so 
far have been limited.

Exhibit 8: UK Growth Forecasts Have Drifted Higher

Source: Bloomberg, Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   6
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 7

10. Our strongest global view is that central banks will deliver fewer hikes than currently 
priced.  In addition, our rates strategists flag underweight positioning, diminished 
technical risks, and risk asset pressures as reasons why the recent rally might extend.  
The market’s narrow breadth makes our equity strategists wary of momentum factor 
exposure, but they still expect the S&P 500 to resume its climb after the midterm 
election, as earnings continue to grow solidly.  Our credit strategists remain cautious on 
IG technology bonds but see scope for some near-term reversal in HY given the scale of 
the recent selloff.  Our FX strategists expect the Japanese yen to act as a hedge against 
further AI jitters, in part because such jitters would shrink US-Japan interest rate 
differentials.  And our commodity strategists recommend targeted exposures to oil 
products and European natural gas to hedge near-term escalation risks alongside 
longer-term exposures to copper, gold, and select power markets to benefit from 
structural supply/demand imbalances. 
Jan Hatzius

Document 394: National Bank of Poland to hold rates as inflation pressures remain contained

Source PDF: all report pdf/National Bank of Poland to hold rates as inflation pressures remain contained.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

The NBP is likely to leave rates at 3.75% on 4 November as
underlying inﬂation pressures remain contained. Attention will
focus on the new inﬂation projection and energy price risks, but
we expect rates to stay unchanged through the end of 2026
before 50bp of hikes in 1Q27

Rafal Benecki and Adam Antoniak
The National Bank of Poland is likely to leave rates at 3.75% at its meeting this
week
The National Bank of Poland (NBP) is expected to keep interest rates
unchanged on Wednesday, with the reference rate remaining at
3.75%. Although preliminary data showed October inﬂation at the
upper limit of the tolerance band around the central bank’s target,
the composition of price growth does not suggest that inﬂationary
pressures are becoming more widespread. Core inﬂation excluding
food and energy likely fell below 3%, giving the Monetary Policy
Council (MP) room to “wait and see” and continue to monitor
domestic and global developments before taking further action.

### Page 2

The new macroeconomic projection will be an important part of the
discussion at the Council’s November meeting. If it points to
elevated inﬂation around the turn of 2027 and 2028, the NBP may
adopt a more hawkish tone. The scale of regulated-price increases
from the beginning of 2027 – particularly for gas – will also be
important, as these could lift the entire inﬂation path. However,
government representatives have already signalled possible
intervention in the gas market, on top of the existing price cap on

gasoline prices.
Our core inﬂation model presents a benign core inﬂation path when
the energy shock expires somewhere in 2027, with the main anchor
being contained wage growth. Labour market conditions are looser
than during the pandemic-driven inﬂation shock and following
Russia’s invasion of Ukraine in 2022. The current government is also
pursuing a tighter wage policy, raising the minimum wage by just
3% in 2027 for the second consecutive year, compared with

increases of around 20% in 2023-24. With wage growth forecast to
remain below 6% in 2027, the risk of second-round eﬀects in
Poland remains limited. This contrasts with the Czech economy,
where minimum wages and public-sector pay are rising more
quickly.
That is why we expect interest rates to remain unchanged until the
end of 2026.
We see hikes in 1Q27, but later and in smaller magnitude than
expected by the yield curve. Our rationale is that a prolonged

energy crisis raises the risk that elevated inﬂation will become
entrenched and the NBP will decide to provide a kind of insurance
tightening.
Further rate hikes by major central banks could also prompt the
NBP to signal its readiness to address inﬂation risks. At the same
time, the risk of PLN weakening is rather secondary, given the low
pass-through from PLN to inﬂation (10% PLN weakening adds
0.2pp to inﬂation).
Our central forecast remains for no rate change in November or

December 2026, followed by a cumulative 50bp of rate hikes in

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
August inflation came in soft...
Inflation moderated in August following July’s strong 
outcome. The CPI rose 0.4% in the month, in line with our 
expectation but slightly below market consensus of 0.5%, 
taking the annual rate to 4.0%. On a seasonally adjusted 
basis, the CPI increased 0.7%mth, underscoring the impact of 
seasonal discounting in August and the start of the financial 
year repricing in July.
More closely watched are the measures of underlying inflation 
including the Trimmed Mean (TM) and market services. 
The TM rose 0.2% in August, in line with our expectations 
but again softer than the market, leaving the annual pace 
unchanged at 3.6% for a third consecutive month. Meanwhile, 
market services excluding volatiles & holiday travel increased 
0.2%mth/4.1%yr. Notable strength (three-month annualised, 
seasonally adjusted) was seen in sports participation (10.9%), 
clothing repair (7.0%), restaurant meals (6.8%) and other 
household services (5.2%). For full analysis of the August CPI, 
see “August CPI – Came in on our expectations ”.
The expenditure-weighted share of the CPI basket 
experiencing positive price shocks eased to 11% in August 
from 15% in July, while the share experiencing negative price 
shocks declined from 17% to just below 13%. The majority of 
the positive shocks were concentrated in consumer durables 
On a three-month moving average basis, the net momentum 
share (positive minus negative) rose to –4.5% from –7.7% 
in July and a trough of –15.8% in May, suggesting the 
disinflationary impulse may be nearing exhaustion. 
... with September due for a moderate pick-up
Looking ahead to September, early indicators painted a 
mixed picture. The NAB business survey showed retail price 
growth slowing to 0.9%qtr from 1.1%qtr in August, with 
labour cost growth easing to 1.7%qtr from 1.8%qtr, while 
purchase cost growth edged up to 2.4%qtr from 2.3%qtr. 
Nevertheless, all measures remain well above their long-run 
averages while the gap between purchase costs and final 
prices continues to widen, indicating businesses are facing 
margin compression (see here for further detail). Meanwhile, 
consumers’ year-ahead inflation expectations held steady 
at 4.9% in September (easing slightly at 2dp) but surged to 
5.3% in October likely reflecting higher fuel prices, with retail 
petrol and diesel averaging $2.20/l (+8.2%mth) and $2.68/l 
(+7.7%mth). We do believe the auto fuel prices are likely to see 
a near term peak in October.  Meanwhile, global shipping costs 
have also surged recently amid renewed Middle East tensions.
Sep quarter unrevised but Dec upgraded significantly
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Neha Sharma, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% (0.62% to 2dp)
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Our seasonally adjusted
estimate increases 0.5% (0.48% to 2dp). For the monthly
TM, we expect a 0.3% (0.26% to 2dp) lift, holding the annual

pace steady at 3.6%. On a 3m/3m basis, the TM is tracking
a 1.0% gain in September (noting that while this measure is
conceptually different to the quarterly TM it still serves as a
useful guide). For market services excluding volatiles & holiday
travel we have pencilled in another 0.2% rise, slowing the
annual pace slightly to 4.0% (a nine-month low).
The largest contributors are recreation & culture (+0.27ppt),
driven wholly by holiday travel & accommodation. Transport

(+0.23ppt) makes a solid contribution, with gains in fuel
(+0.26ppt) partly offset by motor vehicles (–0.02ppt). Housing
also contributes (+0.13ppt), with rents and new dwellings
each adding 0.02ppt while property rates add 0.08ppt.
The key detractors in the month are food & non-alcoholic
beverages (–0.08ppt) and garments (–0.04ppt).
Key details for the month of September
Food & non-alcoholic beverages are expected to fall
0.5%mth, easing the annual pace to 2.8%yr. The weakness is

concentrated in a 3.7%mth decline in fruit & vegetables, while
non-alcoholic beverages are also expected to fall 1.5%mth.
Meals out & takeaway is forecast to rise 0.1%mth, easing the
annual rate to 3.9%.
Housing is expected to rise 0.6% in September, slowing the
annual pace to 5.5%yr. Component-wise:
• Rents are expected to rise 0.3%mth and 3.7%yr. CPI rent
inflation is nearing its peak, consistent with the moderation
in asking rent growth. We expect rent inflation to ease to

3.6%yr by end-2026 and 2.5%yr by end-2027.
• New dwelling prices, which capture only the building
component of construction costs, are expected to rise
0.2%mth and 4.9%yr. Ongoing weakness in established
housing markets would be increasing builders’ difficulty in
passing through higher costs to new builds. As a result, we
expect new dwelling inflation to moderate to 4.5%yr by
end-2026 and 2.1%yr by end-2027.
Transport inflation is expected to rise 2.0%mth in September,

lifting the annual pace to 7.7%yr reflecting an 8.3%mth
increase in fuel prices following higher oil prices through the
month. Motor vehicle prices and spare parts are expected to
fall 0.7%mth and 1.3%mth, respectively. In NSW, a one-year
vehicle registration discount between $80-100 came into
effect from 1 September, which is likely to weigh on the other
services in respect of motor vehicles component.
Recreation & culture is expected to rise 2.1%mth driven by a

4.4%mth increase in holiday travel, partly offset by a 0.4%
decline in audio, visual & computing equipment & services and
a 1.3% fall in sporting services.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Risks to September baseline
We view the risks around the September CPI outcome as broadly 
balanced. Waning spending momentum outside of fuel may make 
it difficult for businesses to pass through higher costs in the near 
term. However, some of these pricing pressures may be deferred 
rather than forgone. The October ban on card surcharging may 
prompt a greater-than-usual degree of price resetting, allowing 
businesses to recover some of the margin pressures that have built 
up in recent months.
Risks to housing are mixed. Trade materials suppliers continued 
to announce above-average price increases through September 
(around 8%). While soft conditions in the established housing 
market may limit the extent to which these costs are passed 
through to new dwelling prices, they nevertheless present an 
upside risk to housing-related inflation, particularly maintenance 
& repair and insurance costs. Growth in advertised rents also 
continues to ease but these new leases take some time to flow 
through to average rents paid measured in the CPI. 
A kick up in October ...
We have a solid preliminary estimate for the October CPI of 
0.6%mth lifting the annual pace to 4.8%yr. It is true that the key 
driver is an 8.9% increase in auto fuel but we estimate the TM to 
lift 0.4%mth/3.7%yr highlighting broader inflationary pressures. 
We expect that the removal of credit charge surcharges and 
resulting increase in retail prices, at most, will contribute 0.1ppt 
but only just so see it as more of a rounding error. Nevertheless, 
these changes come at a time of a significant upstream price 
pressure, particularly for SMEs, so there is some risk of broader 
price adjustments. As such, we expect marginally larger than 
usual price adjustments in October particularly in hospitality 
and household services. Being the first month of the quarter the 
October lift sets a higher base for the quarterly average estimates 
resulting in an upgrade to our December quarter estimate.
We have included a table with a monthly profile for the CPI, 
seasonally adjusted CPI and the TM out to June 2027. At 
June 2027 we have an annual pace of 3.7%yr for the CPI and 
seasonally adjusted CPI and 3.3%yr for the TM. There is also a 
table breaking down the monthly components of the CPI through 
the December quarter. 
... highlights stronger inflationary pressure ...
Our September quarter CPI estimate is unrevised at 1.3%qtr 
with the annual pace steady at 3.9%yr. At the margin there 
was a 0.04ppt increase due to a lift in auto fuel but this is 
1.29%qtr at two decimal places so still rounds to 1.3%. 
The seasonally adjusted CPI gains 1.1% in the September 
quarter highlighting that part of the increase is due to usual 
seasonality. Our TM estimate for Q3 has a small downward 
revision of 0.03ppt to 0.88% at two decimal place so our 
published estimate is also unrevised at 0.9%qtr. 
... resulting in an upward revision to Q4 2026
Westpac’s December quarter CPI estimate has been boosted 
from a 0.5%qtr to 1.3%qtr. Driving most of this change from 
our previous estimate is the 15.3% increase in autofuel, it

A disinflationary impulse kicks in for 2027
We now expect auto fuel prices to peak in October (240 cpl 
for petrol, 284 cpl for diesel) and then to trend lower through 
2027 (184 cpl for petrol and 194 cpl for diesel by December 
2027) leading to a 13.6% falling in auto fuel in the first half 
of 2027 subtracting 0.46ppt off the CPI over the same period 
of time. For a detailed description on how we constructed the 
auto fuels profile please see: “ Persistent energy pressures 
have further to run ” 
The first half of 2027 will also see an almost 5% decline in 
holiday travel & accommodation which will subtract a further 
0.29ppt off the CPI. However, outside of that any broader 
moderation in inflation is expected to be more muted hence 
we have the TM printing 0.7%qtr for each quarter in 2027.  
Critical to this moderation in core inflation is our expect step 
down in dwelling price inflation on the back of falling estab -
lished home prices. From a 1.1%qtr increase in Q3 2026 we 
see dwelling price increasing by 0.7% in the Q4 2026, and 
then 0.6% in both Q1 and Q2 2027 before dropping to 0.5%qtr 
in Q3 and Q4 2027. This will see dwelling price inflation in the 
CPI drop from 5.4%yr at June 2026 to 3.0%yr by June 2027 
then down to 2.2%yr by December 2027. 
At least for the near term a key uncertainty remains the extent 
to which businesses are able to pass through ongoing labour 
and non-labour cost pressures and how this unwinds through 
2027 as energy prices ease and the domestic economy slows 
under the weight of higher interest rates and falling estab -
lished home prices. There is a risk that firms face greater 
resistance to price increases if discretionary household spend -
ing softens by more than expected. That said, these headwinds 
may be at least partially offset by the stronger starting point 
for household incomes, which has proven more resilient than 
previously anticipated, and by higher energy prices due to the 
ongoing conflict in the Middle East.
That said, there is also the risk that the disinflationary pulse by 
the second half of 2027 could be greater than what we antici-

Document 434: The FOMC pause to reflect (2026-11-03)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The FOMC pause to reflect (2026-11-03).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

#fomc #usd #us
The FOMC pause to reflect
By Elliot Clarke
Head of International Economics, Westpac
08:14 October 29 2026 Share
No change in October, though confidence in the economy's strength and two dissents point
to a hike in December.
The FOMC held at their October meeting, albeit with two dissents, Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed
President Hammack preferring to hike by 25bps at this meeting. The statement was short and, in the
press conference, Chair Warsh again declined to provide definitive guidance on the near-term policy

### Page 2

Related articles
outlook.
The Committee remains positive on economic activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding
at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in September). Despite elevated uncertainty, “domestic
spending has been resilient”, underpinned by “strong” productivity growth and “robust” capital
investment. Chair Warsh subsequently also emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment. Labour demand and supply were represented as balanced and resilient in

both the statement and press conference.
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains
elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual
consumer inflation to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target.
Along with the two dissents, this guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help the
Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December. Thereafter,

the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through 2027.
Towards the end of the press conference, Chair Warsh also announced a structural change in the
FOMC schedule. While there will still be 8 meetings in 2027, a meeting in the fall will focus on
monetary policy strategy versus the current stance of monetary policy. Chair Warsh then intends for 2
of the 8 meetings in 2028 to have this strategy focus. If realised, this will see the FOMC review and

vote on the current stance of monetary policy just 6 times per calendar year from 2028.

Document 478: What adj EPS and synergies like after Marvell and Lumentum merge_

Source PDF: all report pdf/What adj EPS and synergies like after Marvell and Lumentum merge_.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-03
